### Step 9 - Save manifest, update cost tracker, clean up, and report

```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from graphify.detect import save_manifest

# Save manifest for --update
detect = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_detect.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
extract = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
# In --update mode, 'all_files' carries the full corpus; 'files' is the changed
# subset. Full-rebuild mode populates only 'files', so the fallback handles that.
# root= relativizes the manifest keys to the scan root (same base as the build),
# so the on-disk manifest is portable across clones/machines and a later --update
# matches cached files instead of missing every one (#1417).
#
# Only stamp semantic files (docs/papers/images) that ACTUALLY produced output:
# a detected file whose chunk failed or was omitted must stay unstamped so the
# next --update re-queues it, otherwise it is marked done and its content is lost
# forever (#2015). This mirrors the library extract path exactly
# (cli._stamped_manifest_files + clear_semantic + scan_corpus); do not stamp the
# raw corpus. Code files are always stamped (AST is deterministic); only semantic
# types are gated on output.
from graphify.cli import _stamped_manifest_files
_corpus = detect.get('all_files') or detect['files']
_manifest_files = _stamped_manifest_files(_corpus, extract, Path('INPUT_PATH'))
# Files dispatched this run (the changed subset) but NOT stamped above still carry
# a stale semantic_hash from a prior run; clear it so detect_incremental re-queues
# them instead of reading them as unchanged (#1948).
_sem_types = ('document', 'paper', 'image')
_dispatched = {f for t, fl in detect['files'].items() if t in _sem_types for f in fl}
_stamped = {f for fl in _manifest_files.values() for f in fl}
_cleared = _dispatched - _stamped
# scan_corpus = the RAW full corpus (not the stamp-filtered subset) so in-root
# files newly excluded since last run are dropped rather than masquerading as
# deletions; untouched files' prior rows are still preserved (#1908).
_scan = {f for fl in _corpus.values() for f in fl}
save_manifest(_manifest_files, root='INPUT_PATH', scan_corpus=_scan, clear_semantic=_cleared or None)

# Update cumulative cost tracker
input_tok = extract.get('input_tokens', 0)
output_tok = extract.get('output_tokens', 0)

cost_path = Path('graphify-out/cost.json')
if cost_path.exists():
    cost = json.loads(cost_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
else:
    cost = {'runs': [], 'total_input_tokens': 0, 'total_output_tokens': 0}

cost['runs'].append({
    'date': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    'input_tokens': input_tok,
    'output_tokens': output_tok,
    'files': detect.get('total_files', 0),
})
cost['total_input_tokens'] += input_tok
cost['total_output_tokens'] += output_tok
cost_path.write_text(json.dumps(cost, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")

print(f'This run: {input_tok:,} input tokens, {output_tok:,} output tokens')
print(f'All time: {cost[\"total_input_tokens\"]:,} input, {cost[\"total_output_tokens\"]:,} output ({len(cost[\"runs\"])} runs)')
"
rm -f graphify-out/.graphify_detect.json graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json graphify-out/.graphify_ast.json graphify-out/.graphify_semantic.json graphify-out/.graphify_analysis.json
find graphify-out -maxdepth 1 -name '.graphify_chunk_*.json' -delete 2>/dev/null
rm -f graphify-out/.needs_update 2>/dev/null || true
```

Replace INPUT_PATH with the actual path (same value used in Steps 4-5) so the manifest is relativized to the scan root.

Tell the user (omit the obsidian line unless --obsidian was given):
```
Graph complete. Outputs in PATH_TO_DIR/graphify-out/

  graph.html            - interactive graph, open in browser
  GRAPH_REPORT.md       - audit report
  graph.json            - raw graph data
  obsidian/             - Obsidian vault (only if --obsidian was given)
```

If graphify saved you time, consider supporting it: https://github.com/sponsors/safishamsi

Replace PATH_TO_DIR with the actual absolute path of the directory that was processed.

Then paste these sections from GRAPH_REPORT.md directly into the chat:
- God Nodes
- Surprising Connections
- Suggested Questions

Do NOT paste the full report - just those three sections. Keep it concise.

Then immediately offer to explore. Pick the single most interesting suggested question from the report - the one that crosses the most community boundaries or has the most surprising bridge node - and ask:

> "The most interesting question this graph can answer: **[question]**. Want me to trace it?"

If the user says yes, run `/graphify query "[question]"` on the graph and walk them through the answer using the graph structure - which nodes connect, which community boundaries get crossed, what the path reveals. Keep going as long as they want to explore. Each answer should end with a natural follow-up ("this connects to X - want to go deeper?") so the session feels like navigation, not a one-shot report.

The graph is the map. Your job after the pipeline is to be the guide.

---

## Interpreter guard for subcommands

Before running any subcommand below (`--update`, `--cluster-only`, `query`, `path`, `explain`, `add`), check that `.graphify_python` exists. If it's missing (e.g. user deleted `graphify-out/`), re-resolve the interpreter first:

```bash
if [ ! -f graphify-out/.graphify_python ]; then
    GRAPHIFY_BIN=$(which graphify 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -n "$GRAPHIFY_BIN" ]; then
        PYTHON=$(head -1 "$GRAPHIFY_BIN" | tr -d '#!')
        case "$PYTHON" in *[!a-zA-Z0-9/_.@-]*) PYTHON="python3" ;; esac
    else
        PYTHON="python3"
    fi
    mkdir -p graphify-out
    "$PYTHON" -c "import sys; open('graphify-out/.graphify_python', 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(sys.executable)"
fi
```

## For --update and --cluster-only

Both are non-default subcommands. `--update` re-extracts only new or changed files; `--cluster-only` reruns clustering on the existing graph. See `references/update.md` for both flows.

---

## For /graphify query

When `graphify-out/graph.json` already exists and the user asks a question about the corpus, answer from the graph rather than rebuilding it:

```bash
graphify query "<question>"
```

Before traversal, expand the question against the graph's own vocabulary so a wording mismatch does not collapse the answer to noise. If the `graphify query` CLI is unavailable, fall back to an inline NetworkX traversal of `graphify-out/graph.json`. Answer using only what the graph output contains, and quote `source_location` when citing a specific fact. For that vocab-expansion step, the BFS/DFS traversal modes, the `--budget` cap, the NetworkX fallback, `save-result` feedback, and the `/graphify path` and `/graphify explain` flows, see `references/query.md`.

---

## For /graphify add and --watch

Neither is part of the default build. When the user runs `/graphify add <url>` to fetch a URL into the corpus, or passes `--watch` to auto-rebuild on file changes, see `references/add-watch.md`.

---

## For the commit hook and native CLAUDE.md integration

When the user asks to install the post-commit auto-rebuild hook or wire graphify into a project's CLAUDE.md, see `references/hooks.md`.

---

## Honesty Rules

- Never invent an edge. If unsure, use AMBIGUOUS.
- Never skip the corpus check warning.
- Always show token cost in the report.
- Never hide cohesion scores behind symbols - show the raw number.
- Never run HTML viz on a graph with more than 5,000 nodes without warning the user.

hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse Completed
hook: PreToolUse
hook: PreToolUse
hook: PreToolUse Completed
hook: PreToolUse Completed
exec
"C:\\WINDOWS\\system32\\cmd.exe" /c "more +250 .agents\\skills\\graphify\\SKILL.md" in C:\Users\nesto\orca\projects\dzik118
 succeeded in 0ms:
```

Only dispatch subagents for files listed in `graphify-out/.graphify_uncached.txt`. If all files are cached, skip to Part C directly.

**Step B1 - Split into chunks**

Load files from `graphify-out/.graphify_uncached.txt`. Split into chunks of 20-25 files each. Each image gets its own chunk (vision needs separate context). When splitting, group files from the same directory together so related artifacts land in the same chunk and cross-file relationships are more likely to be extracted.

**Step B2 - Dispatch ALL subagents in a single message**

Call the Agent tool multiple times IN THE SAME RESPONSE - one call per chunk. This is the only way they run in parallel. If you make one Agent call, wait, then make another, you are doing it sequentially and defeating the purpose.

**IMPORTANT - subagent type:** Always use `subagent_type="general-purpose"`. Do NOT use `Explore` - it is read-only and cannot write chunk files to disk, which silently drops extraction results. General-purpose has Write and Bash access which the subagent needs.

Concrete example for 3 chunks:
```
[Agent tool call 1: files 1-15, subagent_type="general-purpose"]
[Agent tool call 2: files 16-30, subagent_type="general-purpose"]
[Agent tool call 3: files 31-45, subagent_type="general-purpose"]
```
All three in one message. Not three separate messages.

Each subagent receives this exact prompt (substitute FILE_LIST, CHUNK_NUM, TOTAL_CHUNKS, DEEP_MODE, and CHUNK_PATH).

CHUNK_PATH must be an **absolute** path — derive it before dispatching:
```bash
PROJECT_ROOT=$(pwd)  # cwd — where Part C globs graphify-out/ (NOT .graphify_root/scan dir, #1392)
# Then for chunk N: CHUNK_PATH="${PROJECT_ROOT}/graphify-out/.graphify_chunk_0N.json"
```

Subagent prompt template:

See `references/extraction-spec.md` for the exact subagent prompt (JSON schema, node-ID rules, confidence rubric, frontmatter, hyperedge, and vision rules). Load it only here, only when at least one chunk holds a doc, paper, or image; a pure-code corpus has skipped Part B and never reads it. Pass each subagent that prompt verbatim with FILE_LIST, CHUNK_NUM, TOTAL_CHUNKS, DEEP_MODE, and CHUNK_PATH substituted, and have it write the result to CHUNK_PATH.

**Step B3 - Collect, cache, and merge**

Wait for all subagents. For each result:
- Check that `graphify-out/.graphify_chunk_NN.json` exists on disk — this is the success signal
- If the file exists and contains valid JSON with `nodes` and `edges`, include it and save to cache
- If the file is missing, the subagent was likely dispatched as read-only (Explore type) — print a warning: "chunk N missing from disk — subagent may have been read-only. Re-run with general-purpose agent." Do not silently skip.
- If a subagent failed or returned invalid JSON, print a warning and skip that chunk - do not abort

If more than half the chunks failed or are missing, stop and tell the user to re-run and ensure `subagent_type="general-purpose"` is used.

Merge all chunk files into `.graphify_semantic_new.json`. **After each Agent call completes, read the real token counts from the Agent tool result's `usage` field and write them back into the chunk JSON before merging** — the chunk JSON itself always has placeholder zeros. Then run:
```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import json, glob
from pathlib import Path

chunks = sorted(glob.glob('graphify-out/.graphify_chunk_*.json'))
all_nodes, all_edges, all_hyperedges = [], [], []
total_in, total_out = 0, 0
for c in chunks:
    d = json.loads(Path(c).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
    all_nodes += d.get('nodes', [])
    all_edges += d.get('edges', [])
    all_hyperedges += d.get('hyperedges', [])
    total_in += d.get('input_tokens', 0)
    total_out += d.get('output_tokens', 0)
Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic_new.json').write_text(json.dumps({
    'nodes': all_nodes, 'edges': all_edges, 'hyperedges': all_hyperedges,
    'input_tokens': total_in, 'output_tokens': total_out,
}, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")
print(f'Merged {len(chunks)} chunks: {total_in:,} in / {total_out:,} out tokens')
"
```

Save new results to cache. Pass the same SPEC_PATH as Step B0 — it stamps each entry with the prompt that produced it, and a write under a different prompt than the read lands where the next run won't look (#1939):
```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import json
from graphify.cache import save_semantic_cache
from pathlib import Path

new = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic_new.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) if Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic_new.json').exists() else {'nodes':[],'edges':[],'hyperedges':[]}
uncached = [line for line in Path('graphify-out/.graphify_uncached.txt').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines() if line]
saved = save_semantic_cache(new.get('nodes', []), new.get('edges', []), new.get('hyperedges', []), root='INPUT_PATH', allowed_source_files=uncached, prompt_file='SPEC_PATH')
print(f'Cached {saved} files')
"
```

Merge cached + new results into `graphify-out/.graphify_semantic.json`:
```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import json
from pathlib import Path

cached = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_cached.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) if Path('graphify-out/.graphify_cached.json').exists() else {'nodes':[],'edges':[],'hyperedges':[]}
new = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic_new.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) if Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic_new.json').exists() else {'nodes':[],'edges':[],'hyperedges':[]}

all_nodes = cached['nodes'] + new.get('nodes', [])
all_edges = cached['edges'] + new.get('edges', [])
all_hyperedges = cached.get('hyperedges', []) + new.get('hyperedges', [])
seen = set()
deduped = []
for n in all_nodes:
    if n['id'] not in seen:
        seen.add(n['id'])
        deduped.append(n)

merged = {
    'nodes': deduped,
    'edges': all_edges,
    'hyperedges': all_hyperedges,
    'input_tokens': new.get('input_tokens', 0),
    'output_tokens': new.get('output_tokens', 0),
}
Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic.json').write_text(json.dumps(merged, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")
print(f'Extraction complete - {len(deduped)} nodes, {len(all_edges)} edges ({len(cached[\"nodes\"])} from cache, {len(new.get(\"nodes\",[]))} new)')
"
```
Clean up temp files: `rm -f graphify-out/.graphify_cached.json graphify-out/.graphify_uncached.txt graphify-out/.graphify_semantic_new.json`

#### Part C - Merge AST + semantic into final extraction

```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import sys, json
from pathlib import Path

ast = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_ast.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
sem = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_semantic.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))

# Merge: AST nodes first, semantic nodes deduplicated by id
seen = {n['id'] for n in ast['nodes']}
merged_nodes = list(ast['nodes'])
for n in sem['nodes']:
    if n['id'] not in seen:
        merged_nodes.append(n)
        seen.add(n['id'])

merged_edges = ast['edges'] + sem['edges']
merged_hyperedges = sem.get('hyperedges', [])
merged = {
    'nodes': merged_nodes,
    'edges': merged_edges,
    'hyperedges': merged_hyperedges,
    'input_tokens': sem.get('input_tokens', 0),
    'output_tokens': sem.get('output_tokens', 0),
}
Path('graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json').write_text(json.dumps(merged, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")
total = len(merged_nodes)
edges = len(merged_edges)
print(f'Merged: {total} nodes, {edges} edges ({len(ast[\"nodes\"])} AST + {len(sem[\"nodes\"])} semantic)')
"
```

### Step 4 - Build graph, cluster, analyze, generate outputs

**Before starting:** the code blocks below pass `directed=IS_DIRECTED` to `build_from_json()`. Replace `IS_DIRECTED` with `True` if `--directed` was given (builds a `DiGraph` preserving edge direction source→target), otherwise `False` (the default undirected `Graph`). Substitute it the same way you substitute `INPUT_PATH` — do not leave the literal `IS_DIRECTED` in the code.

```bash
mkdir -p graphify-out
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import sys, json
from graphify.build import build_from_json
from graphify.cluster import cluster, score_all
from graphify.analyze import god_nodes, surprising_connections, suggest_questions
from graphify.report import generate
from graphify.export import to_json
from pathlib import Path

extraction = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
detection  = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_detect.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))

# root= mirrors the --update runbook (#1361): relativize source_file to the same
# base so the full build and incremental --update never drift apart on re-extract.
G = build_from_json(extraction, root='INPUT_PATH', directed=IS_DIRECTED)
# Guard BEFORE any write: an empty extraction must not clobber a good graph.json /
# GRAPH_REPORT.md / analysis sidecar. Check immediately after build (#1392).
if G.number_of_nodes() == 0:
    print('ERROR: Graph is empty - extraction produced no nodes.')
    print('Possible causes: all files were skipped, binary-only corpus, or extraction failed.')
    raise SystemExit(1)
communities = cluster(G)
cohesion = score_all(G, communities)
tokens = {'input': extraction.get('input_tokens', 0), 'output': extraction.get('output_tokens', 0)}
gods = god_nodes(G)
surprises = surprising_connections(G, communities)
labels = {cid: 'Community ' + str(cid) for cid in communities}
# Placeholder questions - regenerated with real labels in Step 5
questions = suggest_questions(G, communities, labels)

# Export FIRST and honor the #479 shrink-guard: to_json returns False (writing
# nothing) when the new graph is smaller than the existing graph.json. Only write
# GRAPH_REPORT.md + the analysis sidecar when the graph was actually written, so
# they never describe a graph that graph.json doesn't contain (#1392).
wrote = to_json(G, communities, 'graphify-out/graph.json')
if not wrote:
    print('ERROR: refused to shrink graphify-out/graph.json (existing graph has more nodes; #479).')
    print('If this shrink is intentional (you deleted files), re-run a full build with --force.')
    raise SystemExit(1)
report = generate(G, communities, cohesion, labels, gods, surprises, detection, tokens, 'INPUT_PATH', suggested_questions=questions)
Path('graphify-out/GRAPH_REPORT.md').write_text(report, encoding=\"utf-8\")
analysis = {
    'communities': {str(k): v for k, v in communities.items()},
    'cohesion': {str(k): v for k, v in cohesion.items()},
    'gods': gods,
    'surprises': surprises,
    'questions': questions,
}
Path('graphify-out/.graphify_analysis.json').write_text(json.dumps(analysis, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")
print(f'Graph: {G.number_of_nodes()} nodes, {G.number_of_edges()} edges, {len(communities)} communities')
"
```

If this step prints `ERROR: Graph is empty`, stop and tell the user what happened - do not proceed to labeling or visualization.

Replace INPUT_PATH with the actual path.

### Step 4.5 - Graph health check (read-only integrity gate)

A non-destructive diagnostic on the extraction, before labeling. It surfaces edge collapse, dangling/missing endpoints, and self-loops — the silent-corruption modes of incremental updates and AST/LLM id mismatches. Read-only; never aborts.

```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import json
from pathlib import Path
from graphify.diagnostics import diagnose_extraction, format_diagnostic_report

extraction = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
summary = diagnose_extraction(extraction, directed=IS_DIRECTED, root='INPUT_PATH')
print(format_diagnostic_report(summary))
flags = [f'{summary[k]} {label}' for k, label in (
    ('dangling_endpoint_edges', 'dangling-endpoint edges'),
    ('missing_endpoint_edges', 'missing-endpoint edges'),
    ('self_loop_edges', 'self-loop edges'),
    ('directed_same_endpoint_collapsed_edges', 'collapsed (directed) edges'),
    ('undirected_same_endpoint_collapsed_edges', 'collapsed (undirected) edges'),
) if summary.get(k, 0)]
print('GRAPH HEALTH WARNING: ' + '; '.join(flags) + ' - graph may be incomplete/corrupt.' if flags else 'Graph health: OK (no dangling/missing/collapsed edges).')
"
```

Substitute `IS_DIRECTED` and `INPUT_PATH` as in Step 4. If a `GRAPH HEALTH WARNING` prints, surface it in the final summary (do not abort — the graph is still usable, but the integrity issue must be visible, per the Honesty Rules).

### Step 5 - Label communities

Read `graphify-out/.graphify_analysis.json`. For each community key, look at its node labels and write a 2-5 word plain-language name (e.g. "Attention Mechanism", "Training Pipeline", "Data Loading").

Then regenerate the report and save the labels for the visualizer:

```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import sys, json
from graphify.build import build_from_json
from graphify.cluster import score_all
from graphify.analyze import god_nodes, surprising_connections, suggest_questions
from graphify.report import generate
from graphify.export import to_json
from pathlib import Path

extraction = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
detection  = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_detect.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
analysis   = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_analysis.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))

# root= as in Step 4 / the --update runbook (#1361) — same base for node-key parity.
G = build_from_json(extraction, root='INPUT_PATH', directed=IS_DIRECTED)
communities = {int(k): v for k, v in analysis['communities'].items()}
cohesion = {int(k): v for k, v in analysis['cohesion'].items()}
tokens = {'input': extraction.get('input_tokens', 0), 'output': extraction.get('output_tokens', 0)}

# LABELS - replace these with the names you chose above
labels = LABELS_DICT

# Regenerate questions with real community labels (labels affect question phrasing)
questions = suggest_questions(G, communities, labels)

report = generate(G, communities, cohesion, labels, analysis['gods'], analysis['surprises'], detection, tokens, 'INPUT_PATH', suggested_questions=questions)
Path('graphify-out/GRAPH_REPORT.md').write_text(report, encoding=\"utf-8\")
Path('graphify-out/.graphify_labels.json').write_text(json.dumps({str(k): v for k, v in labels.items()}, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")
# Re-export so graph.json nodes carry the curated community_name (#2490).
# Same extraction as Step 4, so the #479 shrink-guard passes on node count;
# if it still refuses, surface the guard message - do not force past it.
wrote = to_json(G, communities, 'graphify-out/graph.json', community_labels=labels)
if not wrote:
    print('ERROR: refused to shrink graphify-out/graph.json (existing graph has more nodes; #479).')
    print('If this shrink is intentional (you deleted files), re-run a full build with --force.')
print('Report updated with community labels')
"
```

Replace `LABELS_DICT` with the actual dict you constructed (e.g. `{0: "Attention Mechanism", 1: "Training Pipeline"}`).
Replace INPUT_PATH with the actual path.

### Step 6 - Generate Obsidian vault (opt-in) + HTML

**Generate HTML always** (unless `--no-viz`). **Obsidian vault only if `--obsidian` was explicitly given** — skip it otherwise, it generates one file per node.

If `--obsidian` was given:

- If `--obsidian-dir <path>` was also given, pass it via `--dir`. Otherwise defaults to `graphify-out/obsidian`.

```bash
graphify export obsidian
# or with custom dir: graphify export obsidian --dir ~/vaults/my-project
```

Generate the HTML graph (always, unless `--no-viz`):

```bash
graphify export html  # auto-aggregates to community view if graph > 5000 nodes
# or: graphify export html --no-viz
```

### Steps 6b-8 - Wiki, Neo4j, FalkorDB, SVG, GraphML, MCP, benchmark (only on their flags)

These run only when their flag is present (`--wiki`, `--neo4j`/`--neo4j-push`, `--falkordb`/`--falkordb-push`, `--svg`, `--graphml`, `--mcp`) or, for the token-reduction benchmark, when `total_words` exceeds 5,000. A default run with no export flags skips all of them. See `references/exports.md` for each one. Run any `--wiki` export before Step 9 cleanup so `.graphify_labels.json` is still available.

---

### Step 9 - Save manifest, update cost tracker, clean up, and report

```bash
$(cat graphify-out/.graphify_python) -c "
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from graphify.detect import save_manifest

# Save manifest for --update
detect = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_detect.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
extract = json.loads(Path('graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json').read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
# In --update mode, 'all_files' carries the full corpus; 'files' is the changed
# subset. Full-rebuild mode populates only 'files', so the fallback handles that.
# root= relativizes the manifest keys to the scan root (same base as the build),
# so the on-disk manifest is portable across clones/machines and a later --update
# matches cached files instead of missing every one (#1417).
#
# Only stamp semantic files (docs/papers/images) that ACTUALLY produced output:
# a detected file whose chunk failed or was omitted must stay unstamped so the
# next --update re-queues it, otherwise it is marked done and its content is lost
# forever (#2015). This mirrors the library extract path exactly
# (cli._stamped_manifest_files + clear_semantic + scan_corpus); do not stamp the
# raw corpus. Code files are always stamped (AST is deterministic); only semantic
# types are gated on output.
from graphify.cli import _stamped_manifest_files
_corpus = detect.get('all_files') or detect['files']
_manifest_files = _stamped_manifest_files(_corpus, extract, Path('INPUT_PATH'))
# Files dispatched this run (the changed subset) but NOT stamped above still carry
# a stale semantic_hash from a prior run; clear it so detect_incremental re-queues
# them instead of reading them as unchanged (#1948).
_sem_types = ('document', 'paper', 'image')
_dispatched = {f for t, fl in detect['files'].items() if t in _sem_types for f in fl}
_stamped = {f for fl in _manifest_files.values() for f in fl}
_cleared = _dispatched - _stamped
# scan_corpus = the RAW full corpus (not the stamp-filtered subset) so in-root
# files newly excluded since last run are dropped rather than masquerading as
# deletions; untouched files' prior rows are still preserved (#1908).
_scan = {f for fl in _corpus.values() for f in fl}
save_manifest(_manifest_files, root='INPUT_PATH', scan_corpus=_scan, clear_semantic=_cleared or None)

# Update cumulative cost tracker
input_tok = extract.get('input_tokens', 0)
output_tok = extract.get('output_tokens', 0)

cost_path = Path('graphify-out/cost.json')
if cost_path.exists():
    cost = json.loads(cost_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
else:
    cost = {'runs': [], 'total_input_tokens': 0, 'total_output_tokens': 0}

cost['runs'].append({
    'date': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    'input_tokens': input_tok,
    'output_tokens': output_tok,
    'files': detect.get('total_files', 0),
})
cost['total_input_tokens'] += input_tok
cost['total_output_tokens'] += output_tok
cost_path.write_text(json.dumps(cost, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding=\"utf-8\")

print(f'This run: {input_tok:,} input tokens, {output_tok:,} output tokens')
print(f'All time: {cost[\"total_input_tokens\"]:,} input, {cost[\"total_output_tokens\"]:,} output ({len(cost[\"runs\"])} runs)')
"
rm -f graphify-out/.graphify_detect.json graphify-out/.graphify_extract.json graphify-out/.graphify_ast.json graphify-out/.graphify_semantic.json graphify-out/.graphify_analysis.json
find graphify-out -maxdepth 1 -name '.graphify_chunk_*.json' -delete 2>/dev/null
rm -f graphify-out/.needs_update 2>/dev/null || true
```

Replace INPUT_PATH with the actual path (same value used in Steps 4-5) so the manifest is relativized to the scan root.

Tell the user (omit the obsidian line unless --obsidian was given):
```
Graph complete. Outputs in PATH_TO_DIR/graphify-out/

  graph.html            - interactive graph, open in browser
  GRAPH_REPORT.md       - audit report
  graph.json            - raw graph data
  obsidian/             - Obsidian vault (only if --obsidian was given)
```

If graphify saved you time, consider supporting it: https://github.com/sponsors/safishamsi

Replace PATH_TO_DIR with the actual absolute path of the directory that was processed.

Then paste these sections from GRAPH_REPORT.md directly into the chat:
- God Nodes
- Surprising Connections
- Suggested Questions

Do NOT paste the full report - just those three sections. Keep it concise.

Then immediately offer to explore. Pick the single most interesting suggested question from the report - the one that crosses the most community boundaries or has the most surprising bridge node - and ask:

> "The most interesting question this graph can answer: **[question]**. Want me to trace it?"

If the user says yes, run `/graphify query "[question]"` on the graph and walk them through the answer using the graph structure - which nodes connect, which community boundaries get crossed, what the path reveals. Keep going as long as they want to explore. Each answer should end with a natural follow-up ("this connects to X - want to go deeper?") so the session feels like navigation, not a one-shot report.

The graph is the map. Your job after the pipeline is to be the guide.

---

## Interpreter guard for subcommands

Before running any subcommand below (`--update`, `--cluster-only`, `query`, `path`, `explain`, `add`), check that `.graphify_python` exists. If it's missing (e.g. user deleted `graphify-out/`), re-resolve the interpreter first:

```bash
if [ ! -f graphify-out/.graphify_python ]; then
    GRAPHIFY_BIN=$(which graphify 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -n "$GRAPHIFY_BIN" ]; then
        PYTHON=$(head -1 "$GRAPHIFY_BIN" | tr -d '#!')
        case "$PYTHON" in *[!a-zA-Z0-9/_.@-]*) PYTHON="python3" ;; esac
    else
        PYTHON="python3"
    fi
    mkdir -p graphify-out
    "$PYTHON" -c "import sys; open('graphify-out/.graphify_python', 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(sys.executable)"
fi
```

## For --update and --cluster-only

Both are non-default subcommands. `--update` re-extracts only new or changed files; `--cluster-only` reruns clustering on the existing graph. See `references/update.md` for both flows.

---

## For /graphify query

When `graphify-out/graph.json` already exists and the user asks a question about the corpus, answer from the graph rather than rebuilding it:

```bash
graphify query "<question>"
```

Before traversal, expand the question against the graph's own vocabulary so a wording mismatch does not collapse the answer to noise. If the `graphify query` CLI is unavailable, fall back to an inline NetworkX traversal of `graphify-out/graph.json`. Answer using only what the graph output contains, and quote `source_location` when citing a specific fact. For that vocab-expansion step, the BFS/DFS traversal modes, the `--budget` cap, the NetworkX fallback, `save-result` feedback, and the `/graphify path` and `/graphify explain` flows, see `references/query.md`.

---

## For /graphify add and --watch

Neither is part of the default build. When the user runs `/graphify add <url>` to fetch a URL into the corpus, or passes `--watch` to auto-rebuild on file changes, see `references/add-watch.md`.

---

## For the commit hook and native CLAUDE.md integration

When the user asks to install the post-commit auto-rebuild hook or wire graphify into a project's CLAUDE.md, see `references/hooks.md`.

---

## Honesty Rules

- Never invent an edge. If unsure, use AMBIGUOUS.
- Never skip the corpus check warning.
- Always show token cost in the report.
- Never hide cohesion scores behind symbols - show the raw number.
- Never run HTML viz on a graph with more than 5,000 nodes without warning the user.

import AdminLayout from "../../../../../layouts/AdminLayout.astro";
import {
  ForbiddenError,
  requireOrganizationAccess,
} from "../../../../../domain/authorization/guards";
import { getOrganization } from "../../../../../domain/organizations/organizations";
import {
  getCurrentOpenPeriod,
  listPeriods,
  NotFoundError,
} from "../../../../../domain/periods/periods";
import {
  getDashboardSummary,
  type DashboardSummary,
} from "../../../../../domain/periods/dashboard";
import {
  getSetupStatus,
  type SetupProgress,
} from "../../../../../domain/onboarding/progress";
import { withRequestDb } from "../../../../../lib/db-request";
import { subtractExact, sumExact } from "../../../../../lib/decimal2";
import StatusBadge from "../../../../../components/ui/StatusBadge.astro";
import EmptyState from "../../../../../components/ui/EmptyState.astro";
import AdminContextDrawer from "../../../../../components/ui/AdminContextDrawer.astro";

const organizationId = Astro.params.orgId!;
try {
  requireOrganizationAccess(Astro.locals.auth, organizationId);
} catch (err) {
  if (err instanceof ForbiddenError) return Astro.redirect("/unauthorized");
  throw err;
}

const locale = uiLocale(Astro.cookies, Astro.url);
const t = (text: string) => translate(locale, text);
const base = "/admin/o/" + organizationId;
const requestedPeriodId = Astro.url.searchParams.get("periodId");

let organization: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getOrganization>> | null = null;
let allPeriods: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listPeriods>> = [];
let summary: DashboardSummary | null = null;
let setupComplete = true;
let setupProgress: SetupProgress | null = null;
let loadError = false;

try {
  summary = await withRequestDb(async (db) => {
    const org = await getOrganization(db, organizationId);
    const periods = (await listPeriods(db, organizationId)).filter(
      (period) => !period.openingReadings
    );
    const { complete, progress } = await getSetupStatus(
      db,
      organizationId,
      org
    );

    // Keep the organization and setup shell available if only the summary fails.
    organization = org;
    allPeriods = periods;
    setupComplete = complete;
    setupProgress = progress;

    let periodId: string | undefined = requestedPeriodId || undefined;
    if (!periodId) {
      const openPeriod = await getCurrentOpenPeriod(db, organizationId);
      if (openPeriod && !openPeriod.openingReadings) {
        periodId = openPeriod.id;
      } else if (periods.length > 0) {
        periodId = periods[0].id;
      }
    }

    return periodId
      ? await getDashboardSummary(db, organizationId, periodId)
      : null;
  });
} catch (err) {
  if (err instanceof NotFoundError || err instanceof ForbiddenError) {
    return Astro.redirect(`${base}/periods`);
  }
  loadError = true;
}

const currentIndex = summary
  ? allPeriods.findIndex((period) => period.id === summary!.period.id)
  : -1;
const previousPeriod =
  currentIndex >= 0 && currentIndex < allPeriods.length - 1
    ? allPeriods[currentIndex + 1]
    : null;
const nextPeriod = currentIndex > 0 ? allPeriods[currentIndex - 1] : null;

const statuses = [
  "MISSING_DATA",

] as const;

const completedSetupSteps = setupSteps.filter((step) => step.done).length;
const nextSetupStep = setupSteps.find((step) => !step.done);

const workbenchPath = summary
  ? `${base}/periods/${summary.period.id}`
  : `${base}/periods`;
const firstMissingItem = summary?.needsAttention.find(
  (item) => item.status === "MISSING_DATA"

const readingsUrl = firstMissingItem ? new URL(Astro.url) : null;
if (readingsUrl && firstMissingItem) {
  readingsUrl.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
  readingsUrl.searchParams.set("dwelling", firstMissingItem.dwellingId);
  readingsUrl.searchParams.delete("focus");
}

function nextActionSentence(value: DashboardSummary): string {
  const { key, count } = value.nextAction;
  if (key === "ENTER_READINGS")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} dwelling still needs a reading.",
      "{n} dwellings still need readings."
    );
  if (key === "GENERATE_INVOICES")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} invoice can be created.",
      "{n} invoices can be created."
    );
  if (key === "SEND_INVOICES")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} invoice is ready to send.",
      "{n} invoices are ready to send."
    );
  if (key === "REVIEW_PAYMENTS")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} invoice is waiting for payment.",
      "{n} invoices are waiting for payment."
    );
  return t(
    key === "CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD"
      ? "This month is closed. Create the next month to continue."
      : "Everything for this month is done."
  );
}

  ? {

      REVIEW_PAYMENTS: "Review payments",
      CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD: "Create next month",
      ALL_DONE: "Open workbench",
    }[summary.nextAction.key]
  : null;
const actionHref = summary

      REVIEW_PAYMENTS: `${base}/payments`,
      CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD: `${base}/periods#create-period`,
      ALL_DONE: workbenchPath,
    }[summary.nextAction.key]
  : null;

type ProgressState = "done" | "current" | "unavailable";
const currentProgressIndex = summary
  ? {
      ENTER_READINGS: 0,
      GENERATE_INVOICES: 1,
      SEND_INVOICES: 2,
      REVIEW_PAYMENTS: 3,

        date: summary.deadlines.invoiceDueDate,
      },
    ].map((step, index) => ({
      ...step,
      state: (currentProgressIndex < 0
        ? "done"
        : index < currentProgressIndex
          ? "done"
          : index === currentProgressIndex
            ? "current"
            : "unavailable") as ProgressState,
    }))
  : [];

// dd.mm.yyyy for overdue details; dd.mm. for the quiet deadline under a step.
const shortDate = (value: string) =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
    year: "numeric",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(new Date(value));
const dayMonth = (value: string) =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(new Date(value));

const manualAdjustment = summary
  ? subtractExact(
      summary.amountDue,
      subtractExact(
        sumExact([
          summary.currentCharges,
          summary.carriedOutstanding,
          summary.lateFees,
        ]),
        summary.creditsApplied
      )
    )
  : "0.00";
const hasManualAdjustment = Number(manualAdjustment) !== 0;

function formatMoney(
  value: string,
  currency: string,
  _displayLocale: typeof locale = locale
): string {
  return baseFormatMoney(value, currency, locale, true);
}

type MissingItem =
  | { label: string | null; meterType: string; reason?: "NO_READING" }
  | { billingRuleId: string; ruleName: string; reason?: "NO_MANUAL_INPUT" }
  | {
      billingRuleId: string;
      ruleName: string;
      meterType: string;
      reason: "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE";
    }
  | { reason: "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES" }
  | string;

function missingReasons(items: unknown[], limit?: number): string {
  const labels = (items as MissingItem[])
    .map((item) => {
      if (typeof item === "string") {
        const legacyReading = item.match(
          /^Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling (.+?)\.?$/
        );
        return legacyReading
          ? fill(
              t("Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling {number}"),
              {
                number: legacyReading[1],
              }
            )
          : item;
      }
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
      if (item.reason === "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES")
        return t("No billing rule applies");
      if (item.reason === "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE")
        return `${item.ruleName} — ${t("no active meter")}`;
      if ("billingRuleId" in item) return item.ruleName;
      return item.label ?? entityLabel(item.meterType, locale);
    })
    .filter(Boolean);
  const visible = limit ? labels.slice(0, limit) : labels;
  const remaining = labels.length - visible.length;
  return `${visible.join(", ")}${remaining > 0 ? `, +${remaining}` : ""}`;
}

type AttentionRow =
  | { kind: "case"; item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number] }
  | {
      kind: "summary";
      key: string;
      label: string;
      href: string;
      actionLabel: string;
      tone: "info" | "warning" | "danger";
      icon: IconName;
    };

const attentionRows: AttentionRow[] = summary
  ? summary.needsAttention.map((item) => ({ kind: "case" as const, item }))
  : [];
if (summary?.unmatchedPaymentCount) {
  attentionRows.push({

    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.unmatchedPaymentCount,

if (summary?.proposedPaymentCount) {

    ),

  });
}
if (summary?.deliveryFailureCount) {

      locale,

      "{n} invoice could not be delivered.",
      "{n} invoices could not be delivered."
    ),

    actionLabel: "Review failed deliveries",
    tone: "danger",
    icon: "alert-triangle",
  });
}
if (summary?.caseStatusCounts.READY) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "ready",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.caseStatusCounts.READY,
      "{n} invoice can be created.",
      "{n} invoices can be created."
    ),
    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=READY`,
    actionLabel: "Generate invoices",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "receipt-euro",
  });
}
const readyToSendCount = summary
  ? summary.caseStatusCounts.DRAFT + summary.caseStatusCounts.PREPARED
  : 0;
if (readyToSendCount) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",

    ),

    actionLabel: "Send invoices",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "send",
  });
}
if (summary?.caseStatusCounts.SENT) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "waiting",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.caseStatusCounts.SENT,
      "{n} invoice is waiting for payment.",
      "{n} invoices are waiting for payment."
    ),
    href: `${base}/payments`,
    actionLabel: "Review payments",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "clock",
  });
}

function attentionPriority(row: AttentionRow): number {
  if (row.kind === "case") return row.item.status === "OVERDUE" ? 2 : 3;
  if (row.key === "delivery") return 0;
  if (row.key === "unmatched") return 1;
  return 4;
}

attentionRows.sort((a, b) => attentionPriority(a) - attentionPriority(b));

const showAllAttention = Astro.url.searchParams.get("all") === "1";
const visibleAttentionRows = showAllAttention
  ? attentionRows
  : attentionRows.slice(0, 5);
const attentionItemCount = attentionRows.length;

// One quiet heading per kind of case row replaces the badge each row repeated.
// Summary rows (counts) carry their own icon and sentence, so they get none.
type GroupKey = "overdue" | "missing" | "other";
const groupKey = (row: AttentionRow): GroupKey =>
  row.kind !== "case"
    ? "other"
    : row.item.status === "OVERDUE"
      ? "overdue"
      : "missing";
const groupHeading: Record<
  "overdue" | "missing",
  { label: string; icon: IconName; tone: "danger" | "warning" }
> = {
  overdue: {
    label: t("Overdue payments"),
    icon: "alert-triangle",
    tone: "danger",
  },
  missing: { label: t("Missing data"), icon: "clock", tone: "warning" },
};
const groupTotal = (key: GroupKey) =>
  attentionRows.filter((row) => groupKey(row) === key).length;
const attentionGroups: {
  key: GroupKey;
  rows: AttentionRow[];
  start: number;
}[] = [];
visibleAttentionRows.forEach((row, index) => {
  const key = groupKey(row);
  const last = attentionGroups[attentionGroups.length - 1];
  if (last?.key === key) last.rows.push(row);
  else attentionGroups.push({ key, rows: [row], start: index });
});
const attentionAllUrl = new URL(Astro.url);
attentionAllUrl.searchParams.set("all", "1");
const attentionAllHref = attentionAllUrl.pathname + attentionAllUrl.search;

function readingsHref(
  item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number]
): string {
  const url = new URL(Astro.url);
  url.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
  url.searchParams.set("dwelling", item.dwellingId);
  url.searchParams.delete("focus");
  return url.pathname + url.search;
}

<AdminLayout
  title={t("Dashboard") + (organization ? " - " + organization.name : "")}
  organizationId={organizationId}
  organizationName={organization?.name}
>
  {
    loadError || !organization ? (
      <section class="dashboard-error panel" role="alert">
        <Icon name="alert-triangle" size="lg" />
        <div>
          <h1>{t("The overview could not be loaded.")}</h1>
          <p>{t("Try again in a moment, or open the billing periods.")}</p>
          <a class="btn-secondary" href={`${base}/periods`}>
            {t("Billing periods")}
          </a>
        </div>
      </section>
    ) : (
      <>
        {!setupComplete && setupProgress && (
          <section
            class="dashboard-setup panel"
            aria-labelledby="dashboard-setup-title"
          >
            <div class="setup-banner-intro">
              <div class="setup-banner-title">
                <h2 id="dashboard-setup-title">{t("Finish setting up")}</h2>
                <span class="setup-banner-count">
                  {fill(t("Setup steps done: {done} of {total}"), {
                    done: completedSetupSteps,
                    total: setupSteps.length,
                  })}
                </span>
              </div>
              {nextSetupStep && (
                <p class="setup-banner-next">
                  {t("Next step")}: <strong>{nextSetupStep.title}</strong>
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            <ol class="setup-banner-steps" aria-label={t("Setup steps")}>
              {setupSteps.map((step, index) => (
                <li>
                  <a
                    href={step.href}
                    class:list={[
                      "setup-banner-step",
                      {
                        "is-done": step.done,
                        "is-next": step === nextSetupStep,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <span class="setup-banner-symbol" aria-hidden="true">
                      {step.done ? <Icon name="check" size="xs" /> : index + 1}
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      {step.title}
                      <span class="sr-only">
                        : {t(step.done ? "Done" : "To do")}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                  </a>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
            <a
              class="setup-banner-continue btn-primary"
              href={nextSetupStep?.href ?? `${base}/setup`}
            >
              {t("Continue setup")}
            </a>
          </section>
        )}

        {!summary ? (
          <div class="space-y-6">
            <header class="page-header">
              <div>
                <p class="eyebrow">{organization.name}</p>
                <h1>{t("Dashboard")}</h1>
                <p class="page-description">
                  {t("Monitor billing, payments and work requiring attention.")}
                </p>
              </div>
            </header>
            {setupComplete && (
              <EmptyState
                ornament
                title={t("No billing periods yet.")}
                description={t(
                  "Create a period to start the monthly billing workflow."
                )}
              >
                <a class="btn-primary" href={`${base}/periods#create-period`}>
                  {t("Create period")}
                </a>
              </EmptyState>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div class="dashboard-page">
            <AdminContextDrawer
              organizationId={organizationId}
              periodId={summary.period.id}
              periodStatus={summary.period.status}
              periodName={formatPeriod(
                summary.period.year,
                summary.period.month,
                locale
              )}
              workbenchPath={workbenchPath}
              refreshSelectors={[
                ".dashboard-month-status",
                ".dashboard-money",
                ".dashboard-attention",
                ".dashboard-more",
              ]}
            />

            <header class="dashboard-header">
              <div class="dashboard-title-row">
                <h1 class="font-display">
                  {formatPeriod(
                    summary.period.year,
                    summary.period.month,
                    locale
                  )}
                </h1>
                <StatusBadge status={summary.period.status} />
                {(summary.period.status === "LOCKED" ||
                  !summary.isNewestPeriod) && (
                  <p class="dashboard-period-context">
                    <Icon
                      name={
                        summary.period.status === "LOCKED" ? "lock" : "clock"
                      }
                      size="xs"
                    />
                    {t(
                      !summary.isNewestPeriod
                        ? "You are viewing an earlier month."
                        : "This month is closed."
                    )}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
              <nav
                class="dashboard-period-nav"
                aria-label={t("Period navigation")}
              >
                {previousPeriod ? (
                  <a href={`${base}/dashboard?periodId=${previousPeriod.id}`}>
                    <Icon name="arrow-left" size="xs" />
                    <span class="sr-only">{t("Previous period")}: </span>
                    {formatPeriod(
                      previousPeriod.year,
                      previousPeriod.month,
                      locale
                    )}
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  <span
                    class:list={{
                      "period-context-compact": !summary.isNewestPeriod,
                    }}
                    aria-disabled="true"
                  >
                    <Icon
                      name={!summary.isNewestPeriod ? "clock" : "arrow-left"}
                      size="xs"
                    />
                    {t(
                      !summary.isNewestPeriod
                        ? "Earlier month"
                        : "Previous period"
                    )}
                  </span>
                )}
                {nextPeriod ? (
                  <a href={`${base}/dashboard?periodId=${nextPeriod.id}`}>
                    <span class="sr-only">{t("Next period")}: </span>
                    {formatPeriod(nextPeriod.year, nextPeriod.month, locale)}
                    <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  <span aria-disabled="true">
                    {t("Next period")}
                    <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                  </span>
                )}
              </nav>
            </header>

            <section
              class="dashboard-month-status panel"
              aria-labelledby="month-progress-title"
            >
              <h2 id="month-progress-title" class="sr-only">
                {t("Month progress")}
              </h2>
              <div class="month-status-lead">
                <p class="month-status-sentence">
                  {nextActionSentence(summary)}
                </p>
                {summary.nextAction.key === "ALL_DONE" ? (
                  <a class="month-status-link" href={actionHref!}>
                    {t(actionLabel!)}
                    <Icon name="arrow-right" size="sm" />
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  <a
                    class="btn-primary"
                    href={actionHref!}
                    data-open-drawer={
                      summary.nextAction.key === "ENTER_READINGS" &&
                      firstMissingItem
                        ? "readings"
                        : undefined
                    }
                    data-dwelling-id={
                      summary.nextAction.key === "ENTER_READINGS"
                        ? firstMissingItem?.dwellingId
                        : undefined
                    }
                    aria-haspopup={
                      summary.nextAction.key === "ENTER_READINGS" &&
                      firstMissingItem
                        ? "dialog"
                        : undefined
                    }
                    aria-controls={
                      summary.nextAction.key === "ENTER_READINGS" &&
                      firstMissingItem
                        ? "admin-context-drawer"
                        : undefined
                    }
                  >
                    {t(actionLabel!)}
                    <Icon name="arrow-right" size="sm" />
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>

              <ol class="month-progress" aria-label={t("Month progress")}>
                {progressSteps.map((step) => (
                  <li data-state={step.state}>
                    <span class="progress-marker" aria-hidden="true">
                      <Icon name={progressStateIcon[step.state]} size="xs" />
                    </span>
                    <p class="progress-label">
                      {step.label}
                      {step.state === "current" ? (
                        <span class="progress-now">
                          {progressStateText.current}
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span class="sr-only">
                          : {progressStateText[step.state]}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </p>
                    <p class="progress-detail">
                      <span class="progress-count">
                        {fill(t("{done} of {total}"), {
                          done: step.done,
                          total: step.total,
                        })}
                      </span>
                      {step.date && (
                        <time class="progress-deadline" datetime={step.date}>
                          {fill(t("by {date}"), { date: dayMonth(step.date) })}
                        </time>
                      )}
                    </p>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ol>
            </section>

            <div class="dashboard-body-wrap">
              <div class="dashboard-body">
                <section
                  class="dashboard-money panel"
                  aria-labelledby="money-title"
                >
                  <div class="money-reconciliation">
                    <h2 id="money-title">{t("Money this month")}</h2>
                    <dl
                      aria-label={t("Money this month")}
                      style={`--eq-rows:${hasManualAdjustment ? 5 : 4}`}
                    >
                      <div>
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>{t("This month's invoices")}</span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd>
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.currentCharges,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true">
                            +
                          </span>
                          <span>{t("Previous debt")}</span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd>
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.carriedOutstanding,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true">
                            −
                          </span>
                          <span>{t("Previous overpayment")}</span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd>
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.creditsApplied,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true">
                            +
                          </span>
                          <span>{t("Late fees")}</span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd>
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.lateFees,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                      {hasManualAdjustment && (
                        <div>
                          <dt>
                            <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true" />
                            <span>{t("Manual adjustment")}</span>
                          </dt>
                          <dd>
                            {formatMoney(
                              manualAdjustment,
                              organization.currency,
                              locale
                            )}
                          </dd>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div class="money-total">
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true">
                            =
                          </span>
                          <span>{t("Total to pay")}</span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd>
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.amountDue,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                      <div class="money-split">
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>{t("Received in payments")}</span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd class="money-received">
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.totalPaid,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                      <div
                        class="money-split"
                        data-tone={
                          summary.caseStatusCounts.OVERDUE > 0
                            ? "danger"
                            : undefined
                        }
                      >
                        <dt>
                          <span class="money-operator" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>
                            {summary.caseStatusCounts.OVERDUE > 0 && (
                              <Icon name="alert-triangle" size="xs" />
                            )}
                            {t("Still unpaid")}
                          </span>
                        </dt>
                        <dd>
                          {formatMoney(
                            summary.totalOutstanding,
                            organization.currency,
                            locale
                          )}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                    </dl>
                    <p class="money-note">
                      {t("Includes invoices that are not sent yet.")}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </section>

                <section
                  class="dashboard-attention"
                  aria-labelledby="dashboard-attention-title"
                >
                  <div class="dashboard-section-heading">
                    <div>
                      <h2 id="dashboard-attention-title">
                        {t("Needs attention")}
                      </h2>
                      <p>{attentionItemCount}</p>
                    </div>
                    {attentionRows.length > 3 && !showAllAttention && (
                      <a
                        href={attentionAllHref}
                        aria-label={fill(t("Show all ({n})"), {
                          n: attentionItemCount,
                        })}
                      >
                        {fill(t("Show all ({n})"), { n: attentionItemCount })}
                        <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                      </a>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {visibleAttentionRows.length === 0 ? (
                    <EmptyState
                      title={t("Nothing needs attention right now.")}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <div
                      class:list={[
                        "attention-list panel",
                        { "is-expanded": showAllAttention },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {attentionGroups.map((group) => {
                        const heading =
                          group.key === "other"
                            ? null
                            : groupHeading[group.key];
                        return (
                          <div
                            class:list={[
                              "attention-group",
                              { "attention-extra": group.start >= 3 },
                            ]}
                          >
                            {heading && (
                              <h3
                                class="attention-group-heading"
                                id={`attention-group-${group.key}`}
                                data-tone={heading.tone}
                              >
                                <span
                                  class="attention-group-icon"
                                  aria-hidden="true"
                                >
                                  <Icon name={heading.icon} size="xs" />
                                </span>
                                {heading.label}
                                <span class="attention-group-count">
                                  {groupTotal(group.key)}
                                </span>
                              </h3>
                            )}
                            <div
                              role="list"
                              aria-labelledby={
                                heading
                                  ? `attention-group-${group.key}`
                                  : "dashboard-attention-title"
                              }
                            >
                              {group.rows.map((row, rowIndex) =>
                                row.kind === "case" ? (
                                  <article
                                    class:list={[
                                      "attention-row",
                                      {
                                        "attention-extra":
                                          group.start + rowIndex >= 3,
                                      },
                                    ]}
                                    role="listitem"
                                  >
                                    <div class="attention-row-main">
                                      <div class="attention-heading">
                                        <a
                                          href={`${base}/dwellings/${row.item.dwellingId}`}
                                        >
                                          {t("Dwelling")}{" "}
                                          {row.item.dwellingNumber}
                                        </a>
                                      </div>
                                      {row.item.status === "OVERDUE" ? (
                                        <p class="attention-details">
                                          <strong>
                                            {formatMoney(
                                              row.item.overdueAmount ?? "0.00",
                                              organization.currency,
                                              locale
                                            )}
                                          </strong>
                                          {row.item.dueDate && (
                                            <span>
                                              {fill(t("Due {date}"), {
                                                date: shortDate(
                                                  row.item.dueDate
                                                ),
                                              })}
                                            </span>
                                          )}
                                          {row.item.daysLate !== null && (
                                            <span>
                                              {plural(
                                                locale,
                                                row.item.daysLate,
                                                "{n} day late",
                                                "{n} days late"
                                              )}
                                            </span>
                                          )}
                                        </p>
                                      ) : (
                                        <p
                                          class="attention-reason"
                                          title={
                                            missingReasons(
                                              row.item.missingData
                                            ) || undefined
                                          }
                                          aria-label={
                                            missingReasons(
                                              row.item.missingData
                                            ) ||
                                            t(
                                              "Readings are required before generating an invoice."
                                            )
                                          }
                                        >
                                          {missingReasons(
                                            row.item.missingData,
                                            2
                                          ) ||
                                            t(
                                              "Readings are required before generating an invoice."
                                            )}
                                        </p>
                                      )}
                                    </div>
                                    {row.item.status === "MISSING_DATA" &&
                                    summary.period.status !== "LOCKED" ? (
                                      <a
                                        class="attention-action"
                                        href={readingsHref(row.item)}
                                        data-open-drawer="readings"
                                        data-dwelling-id={row.item.dwellingId}
                                        aria-haspopup="dialog"
                                        aria-controls="admin-context-drawer"
                                      >
                                        {t("Enter readings")}
                                        <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                                      </a>
                                    ) : (
                                      <a
                                        class="attention-action"
                                        href={`${base}/payments`}
                                      >
                                        {t("Review payments")}
                                        <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                                      </a>
                                    )}
                                  </article>
                                ) : (
                                  <article
                                    class:list={[
                                      "attention-row attention-summary-row",
                                      {
                                        "attention-extra":
                                          group.start + rowIndex >= 3,
                                      },
                                    ]}
                                    data-tone={row.tone}
                                    role="listitem"
                                  >
                                    <span
                                      class="attention-summary-icon"
                                      aria-hidden="true"
                                    >
                                      <Icon name={row.icon} size="sm" />
                                    </span>
                                    <p>{row.label}</p>
                                    <a class="attention-action" href={row.href}>
                                      {t(row.actionLabel)}
                                      <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                                    </a>
                                  </article>
                                )
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </section>
              </div>
            </div>

            <details class="dashboard-more panel">
              <summary>{t("Additional information")}</summary>
              <div class="dashboard-more-grid">
                {setupComplete && (
                  <section
                    class="dashboard-readiness"
                    aria-labelledby="readiness-title"
                  >
                    <h2 id="readiness-title">{t("Billing readiness")}</h2>
                    <ul>
                      <li data-ready="true">
                        <Icon name="check" size="xs" />
                        {t("Setup complete")}
                      </li>
                      <li data-ready={String(summary.readiness.billingReady)}>
                        <Icon
                          name={
                            summary.readiness.billingReady ? "check" : "clock"
                          }
                          size="xs"
                        />
                        {t("All readings entered")}:{" "}
                        {t(summary.readiness.billingReady ? "Done" : "To do")}
                      </li>
                      <li data-ready={String(summary.readiness.readyToSend)}>
                        <Icon
                          name={
                            summary.readiness.readyToSend ? "check" : "clock"
                          }
                          size="xs"
                        />
                        {t("Ready to send")}:{" "}
                        {t(summary.readiness.readyToSend ? "Done" : "To do")}
                      </li>
                    </ul>
                  </section>
                )}
                <section aria-labelledby="case-status-title">
                  <h2 id="case-status-title">{t("Case status")}</h2>
                  <div class="dashboard-status-list">
                    {statuses.map((status) => (
                      <a href={`${workbenchPath}?status=${status}`}>
                        <StatusBadge status={status} />
                        <span>
                          {summary.caseStatusCounts[status]}
                          <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                        </span>
                      </a>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
                <section aria-labelledby="consumption-title">
                  <h2 id="consumption-title">
                    <Icon name="droplet" size="sm" />
                    {t("Consumption")}
                  </h2>
                  <dl class="consumption-list">
                    <div>
                      <dt>{t("Cold water")}</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {formatNumber(summary.coldWaterConsumption, locale)} m³
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <dt>{t("Hot water")}</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {formatNumber(summary.hotWaterConsumption, locale)} m³
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  </dl>
                  {summary.carriedForwardReadingCount > 0 && (
                    <p class="carried-readings-note">
                      {plural(
                        locale,
                        summary.carriedForwardReadingCount,
                        "Includes {n} reading carried over from the previous month.",
                        "Includes {n} readings carried over from the previous month."
                      )}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </section>
              </div>
            </details>
          </div>
        )}
      </>
    )
  }
</AdminLayout>

  }
  .setup-banner-step.is-done .setup-banner-symbol {
    background: var(--color-badge-success-bg);
    color: var(--color-badge-success-text);
  }
  .setup-banner-step.is-next .setup-banner-symbol {
    background: var(--color-primary);
    color: var(--color-on-primary);
  }

  /* One compact row: month + status on the left, month switcher on the right. */
  .dashboard-header {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem 1.5rem;
  }
  /* Never wrap: a wrapped title is what made locked months taller than open ones. */
  .dashboard-title-row {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.25rem 0.75rem;
    min-width: 0;
    min-height: 2.75rem;
  }
  .dashboard-title-row h1 {
    min-width: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 2.4vw, 2rem);
    line-height: 1.2;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .dashboard-title-row > :global(.status-badge) {
    flex: 0 0 auto;
  }
  .dashboard-period-context {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    margin-inline-start: 0.25rem;
    color: var(--color-muted);
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.4;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  /* Segmented previous / next month control. */
  .dashboard-period-nav {
    display: inline-flex;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-border);
    border-radius: var(--radius-control);
    background: var(--color-surface);
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  .dashboard-period-nav > a,
  .dashboard-period-nav > span {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.375rem;
    min-height: 2.75rem;
    padding: 0.5rem 0.875rem;
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.4;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  /* With the sidebar, ~1024px leaves the title only a few px of slack: take it
     from the switcher's padding first so the month name is never ellipsised. */

    .dashboard-period-nav > span {
      padding-inline: 0.625rem;
    }
  }

  }
  .dashboard-period-nav > a {
    color: var(--color-primary);

    cursor: default;

  }

  }

  /* The one focal point of the band. */

  }
  .month-status-link,
  .dashboard-section-heading > a,
  .attention-action {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.375rem;

    font-weight: 600;
    text-decoration: underline;
    text-decoration-color: var(--color-border);
    text-underline-offset: 0.25em;
  }
  .month-status-link:hover,
  .dashboard-section-heading > a:hover,
  .attention-action:hover {
    color: var(--color-primary-hover);
    text-decoration-color: currentColor;
  }
  .month-progress {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0;
    margin: 1rem 0 0;
    padding: 0;
    list-style: none;
  }
  /* Icon + label on line one, "X of Y" and the step's deadline on line two, so
     no step is wider than its column even in Latvian or Russian at 1024px. */
  .month-progress li {
    position: relative;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0 0.5rem;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 0.625rem 0.75rem 0.625rem 0;
    border-top: 2px solid var(--color-border);
  }
  .progress-detail {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0 0.75rem;
  }
  .month-progress li[data-state="done"] {
    border-top-color: var(--color-success);
  }
  .month-progress li[data-state="current"] {
    border-top-color: var(--color-primary);
  }
  .month-progress li + li {
    padding-left: 0.75rem;
  }
  @media (max-width: 45rem) {
    .month-progress li + li {
      padding-left: 0;
    }
  }

  value: string,
  currency: string,
  _displayLocale: typeof locale = locale
): string {
  return baseFormatMoney(value, currency, locale, true);
}

const hasManualAdjustment = Number(manualAdjustment) !== 0;

function formatMoney(
  value: string,
  currency: string,
  _displayLocale: typeof locale = locale
): string {
  return baseFormatMoney(value, currency, locale, true);
}

type MissingItem =
  | { label: string | null; meterType: string; reason?: "NO_READING" }
  | { billingRuleId: string; ruleName: string; reason?: "NO_MANUAL_INPUT" }
  | {
      billingRuleId: string;
      ruleName: string;
      meterType: string;
      reason: "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE";
    }
  | { reason: "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES" }
  | string;

function missingReasons(items: unknown[], limit?: number): string {
  const labels = (items as MissingItem[])
    .map((item) => {
      if (typeof item === "string") {
        const legacyReading = item.match(
          /^Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling (.+?)\.?$/
        );
        return legacyReading
          ? fill(
              t("Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling {number}"),
              {
                number: legacyReading[1],
              }
            )
          : item;
      }
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
      if (item.reason === "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES")
        return t("No billing rule applies");
      if (item.reason === "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE")
        return `${item.ruleName} — ${t("no active meter")}`;
      if ("billingRuleId" in item) return item.ruleName;
      return item.label ?? entityLabel(item.meterType, locale);
    })
    .filter(Boolean);
  const visible = limit ? labels.slice(0, limit) : labels;
  const remaining = labels.length - visible.length;
  return `${visible.join(", ")}${remaining > 0 ? `, +${remaining}` : ""}`;
}

      key: string;
      label: string;
