      actionLabel: string;
      tone: "info" | "warning" | "danger";
      icon: IconName;
    };

function attentionPriority(row: AttentionRow): number {
  if (row.kind === "case") return row.item.status === "OVERDUE" ? 2 : 3;
  if (row.key === "delivery") return 0;
  if (row.key === "unmatched") return 1;
  return 4;
}

attentionRows.sort((a, b) => attentionPriority(a) - attentionPriority(b));

const showAllAttention = Astro.url.searchParams.get("all") === "1";
const visibleAttentionRows = showAllAttention
  ? attentionRows
  : attentionRows.slice(0, 5);
const attentionItemCount = attentionRows.length;

// One quiet heading per kind of case row replaces the badge each row repeated.
// Summary rows (counts) carry their own icon and sentence, so they get none.
type GroupKey = "overdue" | "missing" | "other";
const groupKey = (row: AttentionRow): GroupKey =>
  row.kind !== "case"
    ? "other"
    : row.item.status === "OVERDUE"
      ? "overdue"
      : "missing";
const groupHeading: Record<
  "overdue" | "missing",
  { label: string; icon: IconName; tone: "danger" | "warning" }
> = {
  overdue: {
    label: t("Overdue payments"),
    icon: "alert-triangle",
    tone: "danger",
  },
  missing: { label: t("Missing data"), icon: "clock", tone: "warning" },
};
const groupTotal = (key: GroupKey) =>
  attentionRows.filter((row) => groupKey(row) === key).length;
const attentionGroups: {
  key: GroupKey;
  rows: AttentionRow[];
  start: number;
}[] = [];
visibleAttentionRows.forEach((row, index) => {
  const key = groupKey(row);
  const last = attentionGroups[attentionGroups.length - 1];
  if (last?.key === key) last.rows.push(row);
  else attentionGroups.push({ key, rows: [row], start: index });
});
const attentionAllUrl = new URL(Astro.url);
attentionAllUrl.searchParams.set("all", "1");
const attentionAllHref = attentionAllUrl.pathname + attentionAllUrl.search;

function readingsHref(
  item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number]
): string {
  const url = new URL(Astro.url);
  url.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
  url.searchParams.set("dwelling", item.dwellingId);
  url.searchParams.delete("focus");
  return url.pathname + url.search;
}

import type { AstroCookies } from "astro";

export type Locale = "en" | "lv" | "ru";

// A presentation preference only; never changes organization or invoice data.
// First supported language in an Accept-Language header, by quality then
// order. Used once per browser (middleware sets the cookie), so a Latvian or
// Russian browser lands on its own language. Anything else gets English.
export function localeFromAcceptLanguage(header: string | null): Locale {
  if (!header) return "en";
  const ranked = header
    .split(",")
    .map((part, index) => {
      const [tag, ...params] = part.trim().split(";");
      const q = params.find((p) => p.trim().startsWith("q="));
      const quality = q ? Number(q.trim().slice(2)) : 1;
      return {
        language: tag.trim().toLowerCase().split("-")[0],
        quality: Number.isFinite(quality) ? quality : 0,
        index,
      };
    })
    .filter((entry) => entry.quality > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.quality - a.quality || a.index - b.index);
  for (const { language } of ranked) {
    if (language === "lv" || language === "ru" || language === "en") {
      return language;
    }
  }
  return "en";
}

export function uiLocale(cookies: AstroCookies, url: URL): Locale {
  const requested = url.searchParams.get("lang");
  if (requested === "en" || requested === "lv" || requested === "ru") {
    cookies.set("ui_locale", requested, {
      path: "/",
      sameSite: "lax",
      httpOnly: true,
    });
    return requested;
  }
  const cookieValue = cookies.get("ui_locale")?.value;
  return cookieValue === "lv" || cookieValue === "ru" ? cookieValue : "en";
}

+type MissingItem =
+  | { label: string | null; meterType: string; reason?: "NO_READING" }
+  | { billingRuleId: string; ruleName: string; reason?: "NO_MANUAL_INPUT" }
+  | {
+      billingRuleId: string;
+      ruleName: string;
+      meterType: string;
+      reason: "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE";
+    }
+  | { reason: "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES" }
+  | string;
+
+function missingReasons(items: unknown[], limit?: number): string {
+  const labels = (items as MissingItem[])
+    .map((item) => {
+      if (typeof item === "string") {
+        const legacyReading = item.match(
+          /^Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling (.+?)\.?$/
+        );
+        return legacyReading
+          ? fill(
+              t("Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling {number}"),
+              {
+                number: legacyReading[1],
+              }
+            )
+          : item;
+      }
+      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
+      if (item.reason === "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES")
+        return t("No billing rule applies");
+      if (item.reason === "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE")
+        return `${item.ruleName} — ${t("no active meter")}`;
+      if ("billingRuleId" in item) return item.ruleName;
+      return item.label ?? entityLabel(item.meterType, locale);
+    })
+    .filter(Boolean);
+  const visible = limit ? labels.slice(0, limit) : labels;
+  const remaining = labels.length - visible.length;
+  return `${visible.join(", ")}${remaining > 0 ? `, +${remaining}` : ""}`;
+}

+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.caseStatusCounts.READY,
+      "{n} invoice can be created.",
+      "{n} invoices can be created."
+    ),
+    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=READY`,
+    actionLabel: "Generate invoices",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "receipt-euro",
+  });
+}
+const readyToSendCount = summary
+  ? summary.caseStatusCounts.DRAFT + summary.caseStatusCounts.PREPARED
+  : 0;
+if (readyToSendCount && summary?.nextAction.key !== "SEND_INVOICES") {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "send",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      readyToSendCount,
+      "{n} invoice is ready to send.",
+      "{n} invoices are ready to send."
+    ),
+    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=DRAFT`,
+    actionLabel: "Send invoices",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "send",
+  });
+}
+if (
+  summary?.caseStatusCounts.SENT &&
+  summary.nextAction.key !== "REVIEW_PAYMENTS"
+) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "waiting",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.caseStatusCounts.SENT,
+      "{n} invoice is waiting for payment.",
+      "{n} invoices are waiting for payment."
+    ),
+    href: `${base}/payments`,
+    actionLabel: "Review payments",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "clock",
+  });
+}
+
+function attentionPriority(row: AttentionRow): number {
+  if (row.kind === "case") return row.item.status === "OVERDUE" ? 2 : 3;
+  if (row.key === "delivery") return 0;
+  if (row.key === "unmatched") return 1;
+  return 4;
+}
+
+attentionRows.sort((a, b) => attentionPriority(a) - attentionPriority(b));
+
+const showAllAttention = Astro.url.searchParams.get("all") === "1";
+const visibleAttentionRows = showAllAttention
+  ? attentionRows
+  : attentionRows.slice(0, 5);
+const attentionItemCount = attentionRows.length;
+
+// One quiet heading per kind replaces the badge each row repeated. Immediate
+// failures stay first; routine work is separated as the next-steps group.
+type GroupKey = "urgent" | "overdue" | "missing" | "next";
+const nextStepKeys = new Set(["ready", "send", "waiting"]);
+const groupKey = (row: AttentionRow): GroupKey =>
+  row.kind !== "case"
+    ? nextStepKeys.has(row.key)
+      ? "next"
+      : "urgent"
+    : row.item.status === "OVERDUE"
+      ? "overdue"
+      : "missing";
+const groupHeading: Record<
+  "overdue" | "missing" | "next",
+  {
+    label: string;
+    icon?: IconName;
+    tone?: "danger" | "warning";
+  }
+> = {
+  overdue: {
+    label: t("Overdue payments"),
+    icon: "alert-triangle",
+    tone: "danger",
+  },
+  missing: { label: t("Missing data"), icon: "clock", tone: "warning" },
+  next: { label: t("Next steps") },
+};
+const groupTotal = (key: GroupKey) =>
+  attentionRows.filter((row) => groupKey(row) === key).length;
+const hiddenInGroup = (key: GroupKey, visible: number) =>
+  Math.max(0, groupTotal(key) - visible);
+const attentionGroups: {
+  key: GroupKey;
+  rows: AttentionRow[];
+  start: number;
+}[] = [];
+visibleAttentionRows.forEach((row, index) => {
+  const key = groupKey(row);
+  const last = attentionGroups[attentionGroups.length - 1];
+  if (last?.key === key) last.rows.push(row);
+  else attentionGroups.push({ key, rows: [row], start: index });
+});
+const attentionAllUrl = new URL(Astro.url);
+attentionAllUrl.searchParams.set("all", "1");
+const attentionAllHref = attentionAllUrl.pathname + attentionAllUrl.search;
+
+function readingsHref(
+  item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number]
+): string {
+  const url = new URL(Astro.url);
+  url.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
+  url.searchParams.set("dwelling", item.dwellingId);
+  url.searchParams.delete("focus");
+  return url.pathname + url.search;
+}

@@ -3592,4 +4252,34 @@ export function translate(locale: Locale, text: string): string {
 }
 
+// "{done} of {total}" -> "3 of 5". Values are shown as given.
+export function fill(
+  text: string,
+  values: Record<string, string | number>
+): string {
+  return text.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, k) =>
+    k in values ? String(values[k]) : m
+  );
+}
+
+// Count-dependent sentence. English picks `one` for 1, else `other`. Latvian
+// and Russian look up `${other}|${category}` (CLDR via Intl.PluralRules; Latvian
+// "zero" uses the "|other" entry), so "21 dienu" / "11 dienām" come out right.
+export function plural(
+  locale: Locale,
+  n: number,
+  one: string,
+  other: string
+): string {
+  let text = n === 1 ? one : other;
+  if (locale !== "en") {
+    const category = new Intl.PluralRules(locale).select(n);
+    const forms = [`${other}|${category}`, `${other}|other`];
+    text = translate(locale, forms[0]);
+    if (text === forms[0]) text = translate(locale, forms[1]);
+    if (text === forms[1]) text = translate(locale, other);
+  }
+  return fill(text, { n: formatNumber(n, locale) });
+}
+
 // Shared by every page rendering an Astro Action's error: never shows raw
 // Zod/ActionInputError JSON. When the action failed Zod input validation
@@ -3646,9 +4336,12 @@ export function formatMoney(
   value: string,
   currency: string,
-  locale: Locale
+  locale: Locale,
+  forceGrouping = false
 ): string {
-  return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, { style: "currency", currency }).format(
-    Number(value)
-  );
+  return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
+    style: "currency",
+    currency,
+    useGrouping: forceGrouping ? "always" : "auto",
+  }).format(Number(value));
 }
